# Mutants of maul-anim: name | file | the line as it is | the line as the
# mutant has it (whole lines, without indentation). Each is killed by a
# test or cannot change a result, its reason in a comment above it.

# Clips (mani-0006), 2026-10-14: 60 mutants; 57 killed (13 by tests
# added after the first sweep). Three survive, equivalent:
# clip-clamp-high: a time past the end gives a factor above 1, which the
# factor's clamp brings back to the last sample. trk-find-same: short of
# a 64-bit hash collision. bld-slerp-small: slerp and normalized lerp
# differ below binary32 precision under 1e-3 radians.

# The JSON reader and decimal conversion (mani-0007), the glTF document,
# skeletons, skins, animations, clips and spring bones (mani-0008 to
# mani-0011) and the arena, 2026-10-14: 150 mutants; 145 killed (47 by
# tests added after the first sweeps). Five survive, equivalent:
# ani-normalized-int: the layout refuses normalized 32-bit components, so
# a normalized output is one of 8 or 16 bits anyway. ani-weights-targets:
# with no morph targets a key holds no values, and an accessor holds at
# least one, so the count check refuses at the same place.
# clp-fold-identity: folding under an identity placement resamples the
# root on the clip's grid, which the clip builder does with every track.
# doc-version-dot: a version starting with "." reads as major 0, refused
# either way. doc-root-object: a root that is not an object has no
# "asset", refused with the same reason, offset and item.
